# Aeras asset catalog and route matrix

Every tokenized asset xStocks and Ondo issue, on every chain, and the conversion routes that fall out of putting the two catalogs side by side. Generated 2026-09-16 from the issuers' own published lists, not from what the app carries today. Companion to `docs/asset-routes.md`, which holds the destination-by-destination status of what is built; this file is the source side, in full.

## Sources

| Source | What it provides | Snapshot |
|---|---|---|
| `backed-fi/xstocks-tokenlist` (`tokenlist.json`, v7.1.0) | Every xStock on eight EVM chains, generated by Backed from `api.xstocks.fi/api/v2/public/assets` | commit 767bfaa 2026-09-15 |
| Backed's `api.xstocks.fi` Solana catalog, via the pinned registry in `operatoruplift/lotline` (`lib/domain/xstocks-registry.json`) | Every xStock Solana mint with issuer ISIN and underlying identity. Fetched from the same Backed API with `network=Solana`, all nine pages, and every mint verified on-chain by that project on 2026-09-12 | commit 7260af3 2026-09-16 |
| `ondoprotocol/ondo-global-markets-token-list` (`tokenlist.json`, v12.0.0, timestamp 2026-09-08) | Every Ondo token on Ethereum and BNB Chain | commit 4ae87da 2026-09-08 |
| `ondoprotocol/gm-solana-simulator` (`constants.rs`, `GM_TOKENS`) | Every Ondo Solana mint | commit 0688add 2026-09-04 |
| `DefiLlama/DefiLlama-Adapters` (`projects/backed`, `projects/dinari`, `projects/ondo-global-markets`) | Backed's bToken contracts, Dinari's dShare factories per chain, an independent 203-per-chain Ondo cross-check | main, 2026-09-16 |
| This repo: `lib/jupiter/xstocks.ts`, `lib/trustware/equivalents.ts`, `lib/ondo/collateral.ts`, `lib/morpho/gold-sources.ts` | What the app carries today | branch `claude/platform-asset-routes-v83ho1` |

Every address the app already hardcodes was checked against these lists: all 18 Solana xStock mints, all 5 Ondo Solana mints, all 9 Ondo Ethereum addresses and all 5 xStock EVM addresses match. The issuers' own sites, Jupiter's token API and CoinGecko were unreachable from this environment, so the GitHub token lists are the record. Both are the files the issuers themselves publish for integrators, and Ondo's Solana list is the one Ondo ships to wallets for transaction simulation.

## Numbers

| | xStocks (Backed) | Ondo |
|---|---|---|
| Tickers issued anywhere | 837 | 452 equities and ETFs, plus USDon and USDY |
| On Solana | 832 | 443 |
| On Ethereum | 803 | 452 |
| On BNB Chain | 775 | 452 |
| On other EVM chains | Arbitrum 175, Optimism 229, HyperEVM 304, Mantle 462, X Layer 803, Ink 774 | none listed (HyperEVM reachable by Ondo's bridge only) |
| Solana only, no EVM deployment | 33 | 2 |
| EVM only, no Solana deployment | 5 | 11 |
| Hong Kong listed, keyed by exchange code | 80 | 0 |
| Tickers both issuers carry | 222 | |
| Of those, both on Solana | 220 | |
| ETF or fund rows, by name (heuristic) | 36 | 117 |
| In the app's buy catalog today | 18 | 0 |

Decimals and programs differ per issuer and per chain, and they are the first thing a route gets wrong:

- xStocks on Solana: Token-2022, 8 decimals, **with the ScaledUiAmount extension**. The raw amount stays fixed and a multiplier moves on corporate actions, so displayed units are raw times multiplier. Nothing in this repo reads the multiplier; every catalog row is priced as plain 8 decimals. That is a live correctness gap the day Backed applies a split to a listed name.
- xStocks on EVM: 18 decimals, and **the same contract address on every EVM chain** for all 804 symbols. One address column covers all eight chains.
- Ondo on Solana: Token-2022, 9 decimals, also ScaledUiAmount per Ondo's program README. Every mint ends in the vanity suffix `ondo`.
- Ondo on EVM: 18 decimals, a different address per chain.

## Which chains the app can sign on

Privy's `supportedChains` is Ethereum, BNB Chain, Monad and Base. A source token on any other chain cannot be converted, whatever a registry says. That decides the route counts below: the Arbitrum, Optimism, HyperEVM, Mantle, X Layer and Ink deployments of xStocks are unreachable until a chain is added in `lib/privy/provider.tsx`.

## Route classes and counts

The destination is the xStock on Solana until Monad xStocks exist. Counts are tickers, not tokens: one ticker is one route family, and a family with three source chains is three routes to test.

| # | Route | Engine | Tickers | Status today |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | Ondo on Solana to xStock on Solana | Jupiter classic swap (Ultra refuses Ondo pairs) | 220 | 3 live (TSLA, SPY, NVDA), QQQ blocked, 216 untested |
| R2 | Ondo on Ethereum to xStock on Solana | Trustware, ETH gas | 222 | 4 quoted, 218 untested |
| R3 | Ondo on BNB Chain to xStock on Solana | Trustware, BNB gas | 222 | 1 quoted, 3 registry, 218 untested |
| R4 | xStock on Ethereum to xStock on Solana | Trustware, ETH gas | 798 | 4 quoted, 1 registry (GLD), 793 untested |
| R5 | xStock on BNB Chain to xStock on Solana | Trustware, BNB gas | 770 | 5 registry, 765 untested |
| R6 | xStock on Arbitrum / Optimism / HyperEVM / Mantle / X Layer / Ink to xStock on Solana | needs a Privy chain first | 170 / 225 / 299 / 457 / 798 / 769 | blocked, not a signable chain |
| R7 | Ondo on Ethereum or BNB Chain to Ondo on Solana, for tickers xStocks does not issue | Trustware, same issuer, not a conversion | 223 | untested; the app has nothing to do with an Ondo-only token beyond hold it |
| R8 | xStocks issued on EVM only, no Solana mint | no Solana destination exists | 5 | none until Backed deploys them on Solana or Monad |
| R9 | Anything above to xStock on Monad | Trustware | 832 once minted | planned, no Monad mints published |
| R10 | Solana xStock or Ondo token to the Ondo Ethereum token, as Ondo Perps margin | Trustware | 7 | SPY, QQQ, SPCX quoted; CRCL, GLD, SLV registry; SNDK blocked |

What to read off those numbers:

- The app's equivalence registry covers 4 tickers. The issuers overlap on 222, 220 of them with both tokens on Solana. Widening `lib/trustware/equivalents.ts` from 4 to 220 is a data change of the same shape, but the registry is keyed to Jupiter Lend borrow vaults today and that key has to go first.
- 230 tickers are Ondo-only. Their canonical Solana form is Ondo's own mint, and no venue in the app takes it. Worth listing, not worth routing until a venue exists.
- 33 xStocks are on Solana with no EVM twin, 80 of them Hong Kong listings (Tencent, BYD, Xiaomi, HSBC and the like). Their only cross-issuer source would be Ondo, and Ondo issues none of them.
- Leveraged and inverse products in both catalogs (TQQQ, SQQQ, SOXL, SOXS, BITX, KORU and the like) are never equivalents of the index they reference. Pairing here is by ticker, so those rows pair with themselves and never with SPY or QQQ.
- Ondo's list carries USDon and USDY, stablecoin-class and not stock routes. They appear in the full Ondo table and in no count above.

## Overlap: 222 tickers both issuers carry

These are the cross-issuer conversion pairs. `Venue` marks what the app can do with the xStock today: JL a Jupiter Lend borrow vault, K a Kamino reserve, B the buy catalog, M accepted Ondo Perps margin. An empty EVM cell means that issuer does not deploy the token there.

## The other four issuers, what the public record says

None of these has a token in this repo. Recorded so the discovery step in `docs/asset-routes.md` starts from an address rather than a name.

**Backed bTokens** (the likely meaning of "bStocks"). DefiLlama's Backed adapter lists eleven contracts: bC3M, bNIU, bERNA, bCOIN, b1B01, bIBTA, bCSPX, bHIGH, bERNX, bZPR1 and bNVDA (`0xA34C5e0AbE843E10461E2C9586Ea03E55Dbcc495`, Ethereum only), counted on Ethereum, Polygon, Gnosis, BNB Chain, Avalanche, Fantom, Base and Arbitrum. Only bCOIN and bNVDA are single US equities; the rest are UCITS ETFs and bond funds. bCSPX tracks the iShares Core S&P 500 UCITS ETF, not SPY, so it is not an equivalent of SPYx. Backed's own repos also carry a testnet Solana `bNVDA`. Confirm this is the product meant before any of it enters a registry.

**Dinari dShares.** DefiLlama enumerates dShares on-chain from a factory per chain rather than from a list: Arbitrum `0xB4Ca72eA4d072C779254269FD56093D3ADf603b8`, Ethereum `0x60B5E7eEcb2AEE0382db86491b8cFfA39347c747`, Base `0xBCE6410A175a1C9B1a25D38d7e1A900F8393BC4D`, Blast `0x6Aa1BDa7e764BC62589E64F371A4022B80B3c72a`, Kinto `0xE4Daa69e99F48AD0C4D4843deF4447253248A906`, Plume `0x7a861Ae8C708DC6171006C57c9163BD2BB57a8Aa`. Calling `getDShares()` on the Ethereum or Base factory returns the full list with the wrapped-dShare twins, which is the discovery call to make. No Solana factory exists.

**Robinhood stock tokens.** No public adapter or token list was found. Arbitrum-issued, and Arbitrum is not a signable chain here.

**Base tokenized stocks.** The only issuers with Base deployments in the public record are Dinari (factory above) and Backed's bTokens. Backed does not list xStocks on Base, and Ondo does not deploy there.

## What still has to be verified per route

A row in these tables proves the token exists. It does not prove Trustware lists it, that a route quotes, or that a Solana pool has depth. The three-step admission test in `docs/asset-routes.md` (list by address, quote at three sizes, check `execution.transaction` is present) applies to every untested row above, and `scripts/trustware-quote-spike.mjs` is the tool for it.
